Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Jump rope with an alternating skip step for a lighter, rhythmic cardio workout.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3679 after decompression) · Source record

A sit-up with the arms crossed on the chest. It trains the abs and hip flexors.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. 3679.json#/instructions/0
  2. Cross your arms over your chest. 3679.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engaging your abs, lift your upper body off the ground towards your knees. 3679.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your upper body back down to the starting position. 3679.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3679.json#/instructions/4

A kettlebell swing with an overhand grip that builds explosive hip power and a strong posterior chain.

3697 — cable kneeling rear delt row (with rope) (male)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3697 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: kneeling.

A kneeling cable row with a rope that targets the rear delts. It trains the shoulders and upper back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach a rope handle to a low cable pulley and kneel down facing the machine. Grasp the rope with a neutral grip (palms facing each other) and extend your arms fully in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3697.json#/instructions/0, 3697.json#/instructions/1

Grasp the rope with a neutral grip (palms facing each other) and extend your arms fully in front of you.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3697.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Attach a rope handle to a low cable pulley and kneel down facing the machine. 3697.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grasp the rope with a neutral grip (palms facing each other) and extend your arms fully in front of you. 3697.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your back straight and core engaged, pull the rope towards your body by retracting your shoulder blades. 3697.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your shoulder blades together at the end of the movement and hold for a brief pause. 3697.json#/instructions/3
  5. Slowly release the tension and return to the starting position. 3697.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3697.json#/instructions/5
